110+  67C3 C9                   RET

  6+  67D5
  7+  67D5              FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT EQU #FFFF
  8+  67D5              IPC_BYTE_TIMEOUT EQU #FFFF
  9+  67D5              IPC_CMD_TIMEOUT EQU #FFFF
 10+  67D5
 11+  67D5              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 12+  67D5              ; Open the single NeoGS package on TR-DOS and read its first sector to #C000.
 13+  67D5              ; The same 256-byte buffer is reused for every following sector while the
 14+  67D5              ; firmware is streamed to NeoGS. The complete file is NEVER staged in host RAM.
 15+  67D5              ; OUT: Carry clear, first sector at #C000, file length and next-sector cursor set.
 16+  67D5              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 17+  67D5              load_neogs_firmware_file:
 17+  67D5
 18+  67D5                      ; Search the exact 8.3 catalogue name neoprog.C.
 19+  67D5 21 9C 6B             ld      hl, neo_fw_filename
 20+  67D8 11 DD 5C             ld      de, #5CDD
 21+  67DB 01 08 00             ld      bc, 8
 22+  67DE ED B0                ldir
 23+  67E0 3E 43                ld      a, 'C'
 24+  67E2 32 E5 5C             ld      (#5CE5), a
 25+  67E5 3E 09                ld      a, 9
 26+  67E7 32 06 5D             ld      (#5D06), a
 27+  67EA 0E 0A                ld      c, 10
 28+  67EC CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
 29+  67EF 79                   ld      a, c
 30+  67F0 FE FF                cp      #FF
 31+  67F2 CA 57 68             jp      z, .file_not_found
 32+  67F5
 33+  67F5                      ; C=#0A returns the directory slot. Fetch its full descriptor with C=#08.
 34+  67F5 F5                   push    af
 35+  67F6 AF                   xor     a
 36+  67F7 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a
 37+  67FA 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
 38+  67FD F1                   pop     af
 39+  67FE 0E 08                ld      c, 8
 40+  6800 CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
 41+  6803 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)
 42+  6806 47                   ld      b, a
 43+  6807 3A 10 5D             ld      a, (#5D10)
 44+  680A B0                   or      b
 45+  680B C2 5B 68             jp      nz, .bad_descriptor
 46+  680E
 47+  680E                      ; Store the actual package length; its header separates the two code sections.
 48+  680E 2A E8 5C             ld      hl, (#5CE8)
 49+  6811 22 AC 6B             ld      (neo_fw_file_length), hl
 50+  6814 7C                   ld      a, h
 51+  6815 B5                   or      l
 52+  6816 CA 5F 68             jp      z, .bad_length
 53+  6819 7C                   ld      a, h
 54+  681A B7                   or      a
 55+  681B 20 06                jr      nz, .package_minimum_ok
 56+  681D 7D                   ld      a, l
 57+  681E FE 03                cp      3
 58+  6820 DA 5F 68             jp      c, .bad_length
 59+  6823              .package_minimum_ok:
 60+  6823                      ; Verify sector count = ceil(file_length / 256).
 61+  6823 2A AC 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_file_length)
 62+  6826 11 FF 00             ld      de, 255
 63+  6829 19                   add     hl, de
 64+  682A 3A EA 5C             ld      a, (#5CEA)
 65+  682D BC                   cp      h
 66+  682E C2 5B 68             jp      nz, .bad_descriptor
 67+  6831 B7                   or      a
 68+  6832 CA 5B 68             jp      z, .bad_descriptor
 69+  6835 32 A6 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_remaining), a
 70+  6838
 71+  6838 3A EB 5C             ld      a, (#5CEB)
 72+  683B FE 10                cp      16
 73+  683D D2 5B 68             jp      nc, .bad_descriptor
 74+  6840 32 AA 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_sector), a
 75+  6843 3A EC 5C             ld      a, (#5CEC)
 76+  6846 32 A9 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_track), a
 77+  6849 21 00 C0             ld      hl, #C000
 78+  684C 22 A4 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_buffer), hl
 79+  684F
 80+  684F                      ; Read only the first sector now. Later sectors are fetched on demand
 81+  684F                      ; by upload_firmware after the preceding buffer bytes have been sent.
 82+  684F CD 6A 68             call    neo_fw_read_next_sector
 83+  6852 DA 63 68             jp      c, .sector_read_failed
 84+  6855 AF                   xor     a
 85+  6856 C9                   ret
 86+  6857
 87+  6857              .file_not_found:
 88+  6857 3E 01                ld      a, 1
 89+  6859 18 0A                jr      .load_failed
 90+  685B              .bad_descriptor:
 91+  685B 3E 02                ld      a, 2
 92+  685D 18 06                jr      .load_failed
 93+  685F              .bad_length:
 94+  685F 3E 03                ld      a, 3
 95+  6861 18 02                jr      .load_failed
 96+  6863              .sector_read_failed:
 97+  6863 3E 04                ld      a, 4
 98+  6865              .load_failed:
 99+  6865 32 AB 6B             ld      (neo_fw_file_error), a
100+  6868 37                   scf
101+  6869 C9                   ret
102+  686A
103+  686A              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
104+  686A              ; Read one TR-DOS sector into the reusable #C000 buffer.
105+  686A              ; Uses neo_fw_load_track/sector/remaining. Advances the cursor only after a
106+  686A              ; successful read. OUT: carry clear; carry set on disk error/exhaustion.
107+  686A              ; Clobbers AF, BC, DE, HL.
108+  686A              neo_fw_read_next_sector:
109+  686A 3A A6 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_remaining)
110+  686D B7                   or      a
111+  686E 20 02                jr      nz, .sector_available
112+  6870 37                   scf
113+  6871 C9                   ret
114+  6872              .sector_available:
115+  6872 3A A9 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_track)
116+  6875 57                   ld      d, a
117+  6876 3A AA 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_sector)
118+  6879 5F                   ld      e, a
119+  687A 21 00 C0             ld      hl, #C000
120+  687D 06 01                ld      b, 1
121+  687F 0E 05                ld      c, 5
122+  6881 AF                   xor     a
123+  6882 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a
124+  6885 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
125+  6888 CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
126+  688B 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)
127+  688E 47                   ld      b, a
128+  688F 3A 10 5D             ld      a, (#5D10)
129+  6892 B0                   or      b
130+  6893 20 21                jr      nz, .sector_io_error
131+  6895
132+  6895                      ; Advance TR-DOS cursor: sectors 0..15, then next track.
133+  6895 3A AA 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_sector)
134+  6898 3C                   inc     a
135+  6899 FE 10                cp      16
136+  689B 38 0D                jr      c, .sector_same_track
137+  689D AF                   xor     a
138+  689E 32 AA 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_sector), a
139+  68A1 3A A9 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_track)
140+  68A4 3C                   inc     a
141+  68A5 32 A9 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_track), a
142+  68A8 18 03                jr      .sector_cursor_advanced
143+  68AA              .sector_same_track:
144+  68AA 32 AA 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_sector), a
145+  68AD              .sector_cursor_advanced:
146+  68AD 3A A6 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_remaining)
147+  68B0 3D                   dec     a
148+  68B1 32 A6 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_remaining), a
149+  68B4 B7                   or      a
150+  68B5 C9                   ret
151+  68B6              .sector_io_error:
152+  68B6 37                   scf
153+  68B7 C9                   ret
154+  68B8
155+  68B8              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
156+  68B8              ; upload_firmware
157+  68B8              ; IN : HL = #C000 reusable first-sector buffer, DE = total package length.
158+  68B8              ; OUT: Carry clear only after custom firmware sends NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK.
159+  68B8              ;
160+  68B8              ; Cold/warm reset path:
161+  68B8              ;   #F4 cold restart
162+  68B8              ;   #20 get total RAM, read three bytes to prove stock ROM is ready
163+  68B8              ;   #14 receive executable bootstrap directly into RAM at $4000
164+  68B8              ;   #13 jump to $4000
165+  68B8              ;   #A1 bootstrap ready, send length, receive #A3 length accepted
166+  68B8              ;   send custom firmware bytes to RAM $0000, receive #A2 firmware ready
167+  68B8              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
168+  68B8              upload_firmware:
168+  68B8
169+  68B8                      ; Only the first sector is resident at #C000. Remaining sectors are
170+  68B8                      ; read into this same buffer as the firmware stream consumes them.
171+  68B8                      ; Layout: WORD bootstrap_length, bootstrap bytes, then firmware bytes.
172+  68B8 22 AE 6B             ld      (neo_fw_package_source), hl
173+  68BB ED 53 B0 6B          ld      (neo_fw_package_length), de
174+  68BF
175+  68BF                      ; Decode and validate the bootstrap length from the package header.
176+  68BF 7A                   ld      a, d
177+  68C0 B7                   or      a
178+  68C1 20 06                jr      nz, .package_header_present
179+  68C3 7B                   ld      a, e
180+  68C4 FE 03                cp      3
181+  68C6 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
182+  68C9              .package_header_present:
183+  68C9 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
184+  68CA 32 B4 6B             ld      (neo_fw_bootstrap_length), a
185+  68CD 23                   inc     hl
186+  68CE 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
187+  68CF 32 B5 6B             ld      (neo_fw_bootstrap_length+1), a
188+  68D2 2A B4 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
189+  68D5 7C                   ld      a, h
190+  68D6 B5                   or      l
191+  68D7 CA 83 6A             jp      z, .upload_failed
192+  68DA                      ; Bootstrap must fit entirely in the first 256-byte sector because it
193+  68DA                      ; is needed before the receiver can be started. The package itself may
194+  68DA                      ; be much larger; only this first sector is buffered at a time.
195+  68DA 7C                   ld      a, h
196+  68DB B7                   or      a
197+  68DC C2 83 6A             jp      nz, .upload_failed
198+  68DF 7D                   ld      a, l
199+  68E0 FE FF                cp      255                   ; maximum length 254 bytes
200+  68E2 D2 83 6A             jp      nc, .upload_failed
201+  68E5
202+  68E5                      ; Firmware length = package length - header - bootstrap length.
203+  68E5 2A B0 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_package_length)
204+  68E8 11 02 00             ld      de, 2
205+  68EB B7                   or      a
206+  68EC ED 52                sbc     hl, de
207+  68EE DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
208+  68F1 ED 5B B4 6B          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
209+  68F5 B7                   or      a
210+  68F6 ED 52                sbc     hl, de
211+  68F8 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
212+  68FB 22 B8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_length), hl
213+  68FE
214+  68FE                      ; Bootstrap and the beginning of firmware follow the two-byte header
215+  68FE                      ; in the first sector. The firmware pointer is advanced as bytes go.
216+  68FE 2A AE 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_package_source)
217+  6901 23                   inc     hl
218+  6902 23                   inc     hl
219+  6903 22 B2 6B             ld      (neo_fw_bootstrap_source), hl
220+  6906 ED 5B B4 6B          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
221+  690A 19                   add     hl, de
222+  690B 22 B6 6B             ld      (neo_fw_source), hl
223+  690E
224+  690E                      ; Number of firmware bytes remaining in the first sector:
225+  690E                      ; 256 - (2-byte header + bootstrap length) = 254 - bootstrap length.
226+  690E 21 FE 00             ld      hl, 254
227+  6911 B7                   or      a
228+  6912 ED 52                sbc     hl, de
229+  6914 22 A7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_buffer_remaining), hl
230+  6917
231+  6917                      ; Firmware low-window image must fit in $0000-$3FFF; stack is now in reserved WIN3.
232+  6917 2A B8 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_length)
233+  691A 7C                   ld      a, h
234+  691B B7                   or      a
235+  691C C2 26 69             jp      nz, .firmware_high_nonzero
236+  691F 7D                   ld      a, l
237+  6920 B7                   or      a
238+  6921 CA 83 6A             jp      z, .upload_failed
239+  6924 18 0D                jr      .firmware_length_valid
240+  6926              .firmware_high_nonzero:
241+  6926 7C                   ld      a, h
242+  6927 FE 40                cp      #40
243+  6929 38 08                jr      c, .firmware_length_valid
244+  692B C2 83 6A             jp      nz, .upload_failed
245+  692E 7D                   ld      a, l
246+  692F B7                   or      a
247+  6930 C2 83 6A             jp      nz, .upload_failed
248+  6933              .firmware_length_valid:
249+  6933 AF                   xor     a
250+  6934 32 C4 6B             ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent), a
251+  6937 32 C5 6B             ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent+1), a
252+  693A 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
253+  693D DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
254+  693F 32 C6 6B             ld      (neo_fw_initial_status), a
255+  6942 32 C7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
256+  6945
257+  6945                      ; Phase 1: documented GS105a cold restart. Also returns a running
258+  6945                      ; Project 124 firmware to the stock ROM before the new bootstrap.
259+  6945 3E 01                ld      a, 1
260+  6947 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
261+  694A 3E F4                ld      a, GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART
262+  694C CD AA 6A             call    gs_boot_send_command_long
263+  694F DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
264+  6952
265+  6952                      ; Phase 2: #20 returns three RAM-size bytes only after stock ROM is up.
266+  6952                      ; Retry #F4 once: if the previous run was interrupted during DMA, the
267+  6952                      ; first #F4 may have been consumed as the DMA end marker; the second
268+  6952                      ; reaches the command dispatcher after it returns to its main loop.
269+  6952 3E 02                ld      a, 2
270+  6954 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
271+  6957 CD 85 6A             call    gs_boot_probe_rom
272+  695A 30 18                jr      nc, .stock_rom_ready
273+  695C 3E 01                ld      a, 1
274+  695E 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
275+  6961 3E F4                ld      a, GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART
276+  6963 CD AA 6A             call    gs_boot_send_command_long
277+  6966 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
278+  6969 3E 02                ld      a, 2
279+  696B 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
280+  696E CD 85 6A             call    gs_boot_probe_rom
281+  6971 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
282+  6974              .stock_rom_ready:
283+  6974
284+  6974                      ; Phase 3: use the native GS105a memory loader, not the MOD player.
285+  6974                      ; #14 RECEIVE BLOCK expects the first (negative length low) byte
286+  6974                      ; before the command, then negative length high, address low/high,
287+  6974                      ; and exactly N payload bytes. The payload is written directly to RAM.
288+  6974 3E 03                ld      a, 3
289+  6976 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
290+  6979 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
291+  697C ED 5B B4 6B          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
292+  6980 B7                   or      a
293+  6981 ED 52                sbc     hl, de                  ; HL = -bootstrap length
294+  6983 22 BC 6B             ld      (neo_fw_block_length), hl
295+  6986 7D                   ld      a, l
296+  6987 CD EC 6A             call    gs_boot_write_command_argument
297+  698A DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
298+  698D 3E 14                ld      a, GS_CMD_RECEIVE_BLOCK
299+  698F CD CF 6A             call    gs_boot_send_command
300+  6992 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
301+  6995
302+  6995                      ; Remaining #14 header: -length high, destination $4000.
303+  6995 3A BD 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_block_length+1)
304+  6998 CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
305+  699B DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
306+  699E AF                   xor     a
307+  699F CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte  ; destination low = $00
308+  69A2 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
309+  69A5 3E 40                ld      a, #40
310+  69A7 CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte  ; destination high = $40
311+  69AA DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
312+  69AD
313+  69AD                      ; Phase 4: transfer the executable bootstrap into RAM $4000.
314+  69AD 3E 04                ld      a, 4
315+  69AF 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
316+  69B2 2A B2 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_source)
317+  69B5 ED 5B B4 6B          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
318+  69B9              .bootstrap_block_loop:
319+  69B9 7A                   ld      a, d
320+  69BA B3                   or      e
321+  69BB 28 0B                jr      z, .bootstrap_block_done
322+  69BD 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
323+  69BE CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
324+  69C1 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
325+  69C4 23                   inc     hl
326+  69C5 1B                   dec     de
327+  69C6 18 F1                jr      .bootstrap_block_loop
328+  69C8              .bootstrap_block_done:
329+  69C8
330+  69C8                      ; Phase 5: #13 JUMP TO ADDRESS. The low byte is the command's
331+  69C8                      ; first argument and must be written before issuing the command.
332+  69C8 3E 05                ld      a, 5
333+  69CA 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
334+  69CD AF                   xor     a                       ; entry address low = $00
335+  69CE CD EC 6A             call    gs_boot_write_command_argument
336+  69D1 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
337+  69D4 3E 13                ld      a, GS_CMD_JUMP_ADDRESS
338+  69D6 CD CF 6A             call    gs_boot_send_command
339+  69D9 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
340+  69DC 3E 40                ld      a, #40                  ; entry address high = $40
341+  69DE CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
342+  69E1 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
343+  69E4
344+  69E4                      ; Phase 6: bootstrap has enabled RAM-over-ROM and is waiting at $0000.
345+  69E4 3E 06                ld      a, 6
346+  69E6 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
347+  69E9 CD 42 6B             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
348+  69EC DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
349+  69EF FE A1                cp      NGS_BOOTSTRAP_READY_ACK
350+  69F1 C2 83 6A             jp      nz, .upload_failed
351+  69F4
352+  69F4                      ; Phase 7: send firmware length low/high and require ACK #A3.
353+  69F4 3E 07                ld      a, 7
354+  69F6 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
355+  69F9 2A B8 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_length)
356+  69FC 7D                   ld      a, l
357+  69FD CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
358+  6A00 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
359+  6A03 7C                   ld      a, h
360+  6A04 CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
361+  6A07 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
362+  6A0A CD 42 6B             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
363+  6A0D DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
364+  6A10 FE A3                cp      NGS_BOOTSTRAP_LENGTH_ACK
365+  6A12 C2 83 6A             jp      nz, .upload_failed
366+  6A15
367+  6A15                      ; Phase 8: stream the custom firmware to RAM address $0000.
368+  6A15 3E 08                ld      a, 8
369+  6A17 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
370+  6A1A 2A B6 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_source)
371+  6A1D ED 5B B8 6B          ld      de, (neo_fw_length)
372+  6A21              .firmware_stream_loop:
373+  6A21 7A                   ld      a, d
374+  6A22 B3                   or      e
375+  6A23 28 31                jr      z, .firmware_stream_done
376+  6A25
377+  6A25                      ; Refill the same 256-byte host buffer only after its current bytes
378+  6A25                      ; have all been consumed. DE (firmware bytes left) survives the read.
379+  6A25 ED 4B A7 6B          ld      bc, (neo_fw_buffer_remaining)
380+  6A29 78                   ld      a, b
381+  6A2A B1                   or      c
382+  6A2B 20 11                jr      nz, .firmware_buffer_ready
383+  6A2D D5                   push    de
384+  6A2E CD 6A 68             call    neo_fw_read_next_sector
385+  6A31 D1                   pop     de
386+  6A32 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
387+  6A35 21 00 01             ld      hl, 256
388+  6A38 22 A7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_buffer_remaining), hl
389+  6A3B 21 00 C0             ld      hl, #C000
390+  6A3E              .firmware_buffer_ready:
391+  6A3E 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
392+  6A3F CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
393+  6A42 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
394+  6A45 23                   inc     hl
395+  6A46 1B                   dec     de
396+  6A47 22 B6 6B             ld      (neo_fw_source), hl
397+  6A4A 2A A7 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_buffer_remaining)
398+  6A4D 2B                   dec     hl
399+  6A4E 22 A7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_buffer_remaining), hl
400+  6A51 2A B6 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_source)
401+  6A54 18 CB                jr      .firmware_stream_loop
402+  6A56
403+  6A56              .firmware_stream_done:
404+  6A56                      ; Phase 9: new firmware has jumped to $0000 and sends ACK #A2.
405+  6A56 3E 09                ld      a, 9
406+  6A58 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
407+  6A5B CD 42 6B             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
408+  6A5E DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
409+  6A61 FE A2                cp      NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK
410+  6A63 C2 83 6A             jp      nz, .upload_failed
411+  6A66
412+  6A66                      ; Cross-check every GS data byte acknowledged by DBIT. The count
413+  6A66                      ; includes #14's remaining three header bytes, bootstrap payload,
414+  6A66                      ; #13's high address byte, firmware length (two bytes), and firmware.
415+  6A66                      ; The first argument byte of #14 and #13 is preloaded before command
416+  6A66                      ; dispatch and is therefore intentionally not counted.
417+  6A66 3E 0A                ld      a, 10
418+  6A68 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
419+  6A6B 2A B4 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
420+  6A6E ED 5B B8 6B          ld      de, (neo_fw_length)
421+  6A72 19                   add     hl, de
422+  6A73 11 06 00             ld      de, 6
423+  6A76 19                   add     hl, de
424+  6A77 ED 5B C4 6B          ld      de, (neo_fw_bytes_sent)
425+  6A7B B7                   or      a
426+  6A7C ED 52                sbc     hl, de
427+  6A7E C2 83 6A             jp      nz, .upload_failed
428+  6A81
429+  6A81 AF                   xor     a
430+  6A82 C9                   ret
431+  6A83
432+  6A83              .upload_failed:
433+  6A83 37                   scf
434+  6A84 C9                   ret
435+  6A85
436+  6A85              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
437+  6A85              ; Standard GS105a ROM transport primitives.
438+  6A85              ; Commands use CBIT; data bytes use DBIT. All waits are bounded.
439+  6A85              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
440+  6A85
441+  6A85              ; Probe stock ROM by reading the three-byte RAM size returned by command #20.
442+  6A85              gs_boot_probe_rom:
443+  6A85 3E 20                ld      a, GS_CMD_GET_TOTAL_RAM
444+  6A87 CD AA 6A             call    gs_boot_send_command_long
445+  6A8A D8                   ret     c
446+  6A8B CD 42 6B             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
447+  6A8E D8                   ret     c
448+  6A8F 32 BE 6B             ld      (neo_fw_rom_ram_low), a
449+  6A92 CD 42 6B             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
450+  6A95 D8                   ret     c
451+  6A96 32 BF 6B             ld      (neo_fw_rom_ram_mid), a
452+  6A99 CD 42 6B             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
453+  6A9C D8                   ret     c
454+  6A9D 32 C0 6B             ld      (neo_fw_rom_ram_high), a
455+  6AA0                      ; #20 reports total RAM as a 24-bit byte count. The 48-KiB
456+  6AA0                      ; memory map needs at least four 16-KiB pages (64 KiB total):
457+  6AA0                      ; fixed pages 0 and 3 plus selectable pages 1 and 2.
458+  6AA0 3A C0 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_rom_ram_high)
459+  6AA3 B7                   or      a
460+  6AA4 20 02                jr      nz, .rom_probe_ok
461+  6AA6 37                   scf
462+  6AA7 C9                   ret
463+  6AA8              .rom_probe_ok:
464+  6AA8 B7                   or      a
465+  6AA9 C9                   ret
466+  6AAA
467+  6AAA              ; Long command wait for #F4 cold restart and ROM self-test.
468+  6AAA              gs_boot_send_command_long:
469+  6AAA 32 C1 6B             ld      (neo_fw_command_byte), a
470+  6AAD 3A C1 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_command_byte)
471+  6AB0 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
472+  6AB2 D5                   push    de
473+  6AB3 16 10                ld      d, 16
474+  6AB5              .long_outer:
475+  6AB5 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
476+  6AB8              .long_inner:
477+  6AB8 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
478+  6ABA 32 C7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
479+  6ABD CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
480+  6ABF 28 0B                jr      z, .long_done
481+  6AC1 0B                   dec     bc
482+  6AC2 78                   ld      a, b
483+  6AC3 B1                   or      c
484+  6AC4 20 F2                jr      nz, .long_inner
485+  6AC6 15                   dec     d
486+  6AC7 20 EC                jr      nz, .long_outer
487+  6AC9 D1                   pop     de
488+  6ACA 37                   scf
489+  6ACB C9                   ret
490+  6ACC              .long_done:
491+  6ACC D1                   pop     de
492+  6ACD B7                   or      a
493+  6ACE C9                   ret
494+  6ACF
495+  6ACF              gs_boot_send_command:
496+  6ACF 32 C1 6B             ld      (neo_fw_command_byte), a
497+  6AD2 3A C1 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_command_byte)
498+  6AD5 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
499+  6AD7 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
500+  6ADA              .command_wait:
501+  6ADA DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
502+  6ADC 32 C7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
503+  6ADF CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
504+  6AE1 28 07                jr      z, .command_done
505+  6AE3 0B                   dec     bc
506+  6AE4 78                   ld      a, b
507+  6AE5 B1                   or      c
508+  6AE6 20 F2                jr      nz, .command_wait
509+  6AE8 37                   scf
510+  6AE9 C9                   ret
511+  6AEA              .command_done:
512+  6AEA B7                   or      a
513+  6AEB C9                   ret
514+  6AEC
515+  6AEC              ; Preload the first argument byte of GS105a commands #13/#14. Those
516+  6AEC              ; handlers read this byte before clearing CBIT; later arguments use DBIT.
517+  6AEC              gs_boot_write_command_argument:
518+  6AEC 32 C2 6B             ld      (neo_fw_tx_byte), a
519+  6AEF 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
520+  6AF2              .argument_wait_free:
521+  6AF2 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
522+  6AF4 32 C7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
523+  6AF7 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
524+  6AF9 28 07                jr      z, .argument_slot_free
525+  6AFB 0B                   dec     bc
526+  6AFC 78                   ld      a, b
527+  6AFD B1                   or      c
528+  6AFE 20 F2                jr      nz, .argument_wait_free
529+  6B00 37                   scf
530+  6B01 C9                   ret
531+  6B02              .argument_slot_free:
532+  6B02 3A C2 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_tx_byte)
533+  6B05 D3 B3                out     (GSDAT), a
534+  6B07 B7                   or      a
535+  6B08 C9                   ret
536+  6B09
537+  6B09              gs_boot_send_data_byte:
538+  6B09 32 C2 6B             ld      (neo_fw_tx_byte), a
539+  6B0C 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
540+  6B0F              .data_wait_free:
541+  6B0F DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
542+  6B11 32 C7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
543+  6B14 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
544+  6B16 28 07                jr      z, .data_slot_free
545+  6B18 0B                   dec     bc
546+  6B19 78                   ld      a, b
547+  6B1A B1                   or      c
548+  6B1B 20 F2                jr      nz, .data_wait_free
549+  6B1D 37                   scf
550+  6B1E C9                   ret
551+  6B1F              .data_slot_free:
552+  6B1F 3A C2 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_tx_byte)
553+  6B22 D3 B3                out     (GSDAT), a
554+  6B24 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
555+  6B27              .data_wait_consumed:
556+  6B27 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
557+  6B29 32 C7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
558+  6B2C CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
559+  6B2E 28 07                jr      z, .data_consumed
560+  6B30 0B                   dec     bc
561+  6B31 78                   ld      a, b
562+  6B32 B1                   or      c
563+  6B33 20 F2                jr      nz, .data_wait_consumed
564+  6B35 37                   scf
565+  6B36 C9                   ret
566+  6B37              .data_consumed:
567+  6B37 E5                   push    hl
568+  6B38 2A C4 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_bytes_sent)
569+  6B3B 23                   inc     hl
570+  6B3C 22 C4 6B             ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent), hl
571+  6B3F E1                   pop     hl
572+  6B40 B7                   or      a
573+  6B41 C9                   ret
574+  6B42
575+  6B42              gs_boot_receive_data_byte:
576+  6B42 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
577+  6B45              .receive_wait:
578+  6B45 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
579+  6B47 32 C7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
580+  6B4A CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
581+  6B4C 20 07                jr      nz, .receive_ready
582+  6B4E 0B                   dec     bc
583+  6B4F 78                   ld      a, b
584+  6B50 B1                   or      c
585+  6B51 20 F2                jr      nz, .receive_wait
586+  6B53 37                   scf
587+  6B54 C9                   ret
588+  6B55              .receive_ready:
589+  6B55 DB B3                in      a, (GSDAT)
590+  6B57 32 C3 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_rx_byte), a
591+  6B5A B7                   or      a
592+  6B5B C9                   ret

595+  6B5C              ; Project 124 private-protocol waits (custom firmware already running).
596+  6B5C              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------

599+  6B5F              .cmd_wait:

601+  6B61 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a

604+  6B66 78                   ld      a, b

# file opened: x86_loader.asm
  1+  6BC9              ; =========================================================================
  2+  6BC9              ; PROJECT 124 — TR-DOS task loader + NeoGS DMA / IPC
  3+  6BC9              ; Hardware protocol is based on NeoGS Programmer's Guide v0.1.A and
  4+  6BC9              ; ports.inc v0.3. The application-level command IDs/frame are Project 124.
  5+  6BC9              ;
  6+  6BC9              ; DMA mapping used here (proven working configuration):
  7+  6BC9              ;   NeoGS physical DMA destination = $00D000
  8+  6BC9              ;   NeoGS Z80 logical DMA frame address = $5000 (default WIN1 page #03)
  9+  6BC9              ;   two-byte length header at $5000; x86 execution begins at $5002.
 10+  6BC9              ;
 11+  6BC9              ; Host DMA writes to Spectrum address $0000-$3FFF. For DMA WRITE there is
 12+  6BC9              ; NO dummy write. The dummy access described by the user is only for DMA READ.
 13+  6BC9              ; =========================================================================
 14+  6BC9
 15+  6BC9              ; neogs_io.inc is included once by neogs_driver.asm before this module.
 16+  6BC9
 17+  6BC9              DMA_HOST_WINDOW    EQU #0000
 18+  6BC9              DMA_WINDOW_LIMIT   EQU #3000
 19+  6BC9

 24+  6BD3 31 FF BF             ld      sp, #BFFF              ; buffer starts at #C000; stack below it

183+  6D5F CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
183+  6D62 E1          >                    POP     HL
183+  6D63             >                ENDIF
184+  6D63                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_guest_alu8_logic_pass
184+  6D63             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
184+  6D63 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
184+  6D64 21 71 76    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_int_guest_alu8_logic_pass
184+  6D67 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
184+  6D6A E1          >                    POP     HL

185+  6D6B             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
185+  6D6B E5          >                    PUSH    HL
185+  6D6C 21 C3 76    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_int_guest_alu8_memory_pass
185+  6D6F CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
185+  6D72 E1          >                    POP     HL
185+  6D73             >                ENDIF

186+  6D73             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
186+  6D73 E5          >                    PUSH    HL

186+  6D77 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
186+  6D7A E1          >                    POP     HL
186+  6D7B             >                ENDIF

187+  6D7B E5          >                    PUSH    HL
187+  6D7C 21 9C 77    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_int_guest_mov_memory_pass
187+  6D7F CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
187+  6D82 E1          >                    POP     HL

188+  6D83             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
188+  6D83 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
188+  6D84 21 DF 77    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_int_guest_mov_address_pass
188+  6D87 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
188+  6D8A E1          >                    POP     HL

189+  6D8C 21 38 78    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_int_guest_iret_pass
189+  6D8F CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
189+  6D92 E1          >                    POP     HL
189+  6D93             >                ENDIF
190+  6D93
191+  6D93                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
192+  6D93                      ; Stage 3A: guest INT 10h video BIOS services and live console stream.
193+  6D93                      ; The NeoGS sends each teletype byte over DBIT while this host drains
194+  6D93                      ; the stream to port #EF and the 42x12 6x8 debug window.
195+  6D93                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
196+  6D93                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_bios_video_start
196+  6D93             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
196+  6D93 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
196+  6D94 21 E0 78    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_bios_video_start
196+  6D97 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
196+  6D9A E1          >                    POP     HL
196+  6D9B             >                ENDIF
197+  6D9B CD BE 71             call    neogs_bios_video_test_and_get_result
198+  6D9E DA 33 70             jp      c, .bios_video_transport_fail
199+  6DA1 3A 2E 7A             ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
200+  6DA4 B7                   or      a
201+  6DA5 C2 3F 70             jp      nz, .bios_video_fail
202+  6DA8 2A 2F 7A             ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
203+  6DAB 11 10 1A             ld      de, #1A10
204+  6DAE B7                   or      a
205+  6DAF ED 52                sbc     hl, de
206+  6DB1 C2 3F 70             jp      nz, .bios_video_fail
207+  6DB4                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_bios_video_pass
207+  6DB4             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
207+  6DB4 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
207+  6DB5 21 22 79    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_bios_video_pass
207+  6DB8 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
207+  6DBB E1          >                    POP     HL
207+  6DBC             >                ENDIF
208+  6DBC
209+  6DBC                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
210+  6DBC                      ; Stage 5A: search TR-DOS directory entry.
211+  6DBC                      ; Name #5CDD..#5CE4, type #5CE5, per the working project path.
212+  6DBC                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
213+  6DBC 2A 23 7A             ld      hl, (P124_X86_REQUEST_FILENAME)
214+  6DBF 11 DD 5C             ld      de, #5CDD
215+  6DC2 01 08 00             ld      bc, 8
216+  6DC5 ED B0                ldir
217+  6DC7 3A 25 7A             ld      a, (P124_X86_REQUEST_TYPE)
218+  6DCA 32 E5 5C             ld      (#5CE5), a
219+  6DCD 3E 09                ld      a, 9
220+  6DCF 32 06 5D             ld      (#5D06), a
221+  6DD2 0E 0A                ld      c, 10
222+  6DD4 CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
223+  6DD7 79                   ld      a, c
224+  6DD8 FE FF                cp      #FF
225+  6DDA CA D2 70             jp      z, .file_not_found
226+  6DDD
227+  6DDD                      ; C=#0A returns only the catalogue entry number. Fetch the full
228+  6DDD                      ; 16-byte descriptor explicitly with C=#08 before using #5CE8..#5CEC.
229+  6DDD 79                   ld      a, c
230+  6DDE F5                   push    af
231+  6DDF AF                   xor     a
232+  6DE0 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a
233+  6DE3 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
234+  6DE6 F1                   pop     af
235+  6DE7 0E 08                ld      c, 8
236+  6DE9 CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
237+  6DEC 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)
238+  6DEF 47                   ld      b, a
239+  6DF0 3A 10 5D             ld      a, (#5D10)
240+  6DF3 B0                   or      b
241+  6DF4 C2 EA 70             jp      nz, .bad_file_descriptor
242+  6DF7
243+  6DF7                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_file_ok
243+  6DF7             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
243+  6DF7 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
243+  6DF8 21 66 7C    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_file_ok
243+  6DFB CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
243+  6DFE E1          >                    POP     HL
243+  6DFF             >                ENDIF
244+  6DFF
245+  6DFF                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
246+  6DFF                      ; Stage 5B: validate descriptor BEFORE reading any sector.
247+  6DFF                      ; #5CE8 = byte length, #5CEA = sector count,
248+  6DFF                      ; #5CEB = first internal sector (0..15), #5CEC = track.
249+  6DFF                      ; The DMA frame window is $5000..$7FFF (12 KiB). Its first two
250+  6DFF                      ; bytes are the length header, so executable payload max is $2FFE.
251+  6DFF                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
252+  6DFF ED 4B E8 5C          ld      bc, (#5CE8)
253+  6E03 78                   ld      a, b
254+  6E04 B1                   or      c
255+  6E05 CA DE 70             jp      z, .empty_file_error
256+  6E08 ED 43 26 7A          ld      (P124_X86_FILE_LENGTH), bc
257+  6E0C
258+  6E0C                      ; Reject files larger than the executable 12 KB window minus header.
259+  6E0C 78                   ld      a, b
260+  6E0D FE 2F                cp      #2F
261+  6E0F 38 09                jr      c, .file_length_ok
262+  6E11 C2 17 71             jp      nz, .task_too_large
263+  6E14 79                   ld      a, c
264+  6E15 FE FF                cp      #FF
265+  6E17 D2 17 71             jp      nc, .task_too_large
266+  6E1A              .file_length_ok:
267+  6E1A                      ; Sector count must equal ceil(file_length / 256).
268+  6E1A 2A 26 7A             ld      hl, (P124_X86_FILE_LENGTH)
269+  6E1D 11 FF 00             ld      de, 255
270+  6E20 19                   add     hl, de
271+  6E21 5C                   ld      e, h
272+  6E22 3A EA 5C             ld      a, (#5CEA)
273+  6E25 B7                   or      a
274+  6E26 CA DE 70             jp      z, .empty_file_error
275+  6E29 BB                   cp      e
276+  6E2A C2 EA 70             jp      nz, .bad_file_descriptor
277+  6E2D 32 2A 7A             ld      (P124_X86_READ_REMAINING), a
278+  6E30
279+  6E30 3A EB 5C             ld      a, (#5CEB)
280+  6E33 FE 10                cp      16
281+  6E35 D2 EA 70             jp      nc, .bad_file_descriptor
282+  6E38 32 2C 7A             ld      (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR), a
283+  6E3B 3A EC 5C             ld      a, (#5CEC)
284+  6E3E 32 2B 7A             ld      (P124_X86_READ_TRACK), a
285+  6E41 21 00 C0             ld      hl, TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER
286+  6E44 22 28 7A             ld      (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER), hl
287+  6E47
288+  6E47              .read_sector_loop:
289+  6E47                      ; Read exactly one sector per TR-DOS #05 call.
290+  6E47 3A 2B 7A             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_TRACK)
291+  6E4A 57                   ld      d, a
292+  6E4B 3A 2C 7A             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR)
293+  6E4E 5F                   ld      e, a
294+  6E4F 2A 28 7A             ld      hl, (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER)
295+  6E52 06 01                ld      b, 1
296+  6E54 0E 05                ld      c, 5
297+  6E56 AF                   xor     a
298+  6E57 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a
299+  6E5A 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
300+  6E5D CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
301+  6E60                      ; C=#05 stores its two-byte error result at #5D0F/#5D10.
302+  6E60 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)
303+  6E63 47                   ld      b, a
304+  6E64 3A 10 5D             ld      a, (#5D10)
305+  6E67 B0                   or      b
306+  6E68 C2 F6 70             jp      nz, .sector_read_error
307+  6E6B
308+  6E6B                      ; Advance host buffer by 256 bytes.
309+  6E6B 2A 28 7A             ld      hl, (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER)
310+  6E6E 11 00 01             ld      de, 256
311+  6E71 19                   add     hl, de
312+  6E72 22 28 7A             ld      (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER), hl
313+  6E75
314+  6E75                      ; TR-DOS sectors are numbered 0..15 in this project convention.
315+  6E75 3A 2C 7A             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR)
316+  6E78 3C                   inc     a
317+  6E79 FE 10                cp      16
318+  6E7B 38 0D                jr      c, .sector_same_track
319+  6E7D AF                   xor     a
320+  6E7E 32 2C 7A             ld      (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR), a
321+  6E81 3A 2B 7A             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_TRACK)
322+  6E84 3C                   inc     a
323+  6E85 32 2B 7A             ld      (P124_X86_READ_TRACK), a
324+  6E88 18 03                jr      .sector_advanced
325+  6E8A
326+  6E8A              .sector_same_track:
327+  6E8A 32 2C 7A             ld      (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR), a
328+  6E8D
329+  6E8D              .sector_advanced:
330+  6E8D 3A 2A 7A             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_REMAINING)
331+  6E90 3D                   dec     a
332+  6E91 32 2A 7A             ld      (P124_X86_READ_REMAINING), a
333+  6E94 C2 47 6E             jp      nz, .read_sector_loop
334+  6E97
335+  6E97                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_buf_loaded
335+  6E97             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
335+  6E97 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
335+  6E98 21 8A 7C    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_buf_loaded
335+  6E9B CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
335+  6E9E E1          >                    POP     HL
335+  6E9F             >                ENDIF
336+  6E9F
337+  6E9F                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
338+  6E9F                      ; Stage 5C: transfer actual task. The DMA frame contains a two-byte
339+  6E9F                      ; length prefix, therefore the total transfer must fit in $3000 bytes.
340+  6E9F                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
341+  6E9F ED 4B 26 7A          ld      bc, (P124_X86_FILE_LENGTH)
342+  6EA3 21 00 C0             ld      hl, TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER
343+  6EA6 CD 4C 71             call    neogs_send_dma_block
344+  6EA9 DA 23 71             jp      c, .task_dma_error
345+  6EAC                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_task_dma_ok
345+  6EAC             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
345+  6EAC E5          >                    PUSH    HL
345+  6EAD 21 C7 7C    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_task_dma_ok
345+  6EB0 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
345+  6EB3 E1          >                    POP     HL
345+  6EB4             >                ENDIF
346+  6EB4
347+  6EB4                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
348+  6EB4                      ; Stage 5D: execute and fetch framed result.
349+  6EB4                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
350+  6EB4 CD B3 71             call    neogs_run_x86_real_and_get_result
351+  6EB7 DA 2F 71             jp      c, .result_timeout
352+  6EBA
353+  6EBA                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_x86_result_ready
353+  6EBA             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
353+  6EBA E5          >                    PUSH    HL
353+  6EBB 21 FB 7C    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_x86_result_ready
353+  6EBE CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
353+  6EC1 E1          >                    POP     HL
353+  6EC2             >                ENDIF
354+  6EC2 3A 2E 7A             ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
355+  6EC5 6F                   ld      l, a
356+  6EC6 26 00                ld      h, 0
357+  6EC8                      MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
357+  6EC8             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
357+  6EC8 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
357+  6EC9 E5          >                    PUSH    hl
357+  6ECA E1          >                    POP     HL
357+  6ECB CD 81 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintHex16
357+  6ECE E1          >                    POP     HL
357+  6ECF             >                ENDIF
358+  6ECF                      MACRO_EF_CHAR 32
358+  6ECF             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
358+  6ECF F5          >                    PUSH    AF
358+  6ED0 C5          >                    PUSH    BC
358+  6ED1 3E 20       >                    LD      A, 32
358+  6ED3 01 EF 00    >                    LD      BC, #00EF
358+  6ED6 ED 79       >                    OUT     (C), A
358+  6ED8 C1          >                    POP     BC
358+  6ED9 F1          >                    POP     AF
358+  6EDA             >                ENDIF
359+  6EDA 2A 2F 7A             ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
360+  6EDD                      MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
360+  6EDD             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
360+  6EDD E5          >                    PUSH    HL
360+  6EDE E5          >                    PUSH    hl
360+  6EDF E1          >                    POP     HL
360+  6EE0 CD 81 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintHex16
360+  6EE3 E1          >                    POP     HL
360+  6EE4             >                ENDIF
361+  6EE4                      MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
361+  6EE4             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
361+  6EE4 F5          >                    PUSH    AF
361+  6EE5 C5          >                    PUSH    BC
361+  6EE6 3E 0D       >                    LD      A, 13
361+  6EE8 01 EF 00    >                    LD      BC, #00EF
361+  6EEB ED 79       >                    OUT     (C), A
361+  6EED C1          >                    POP     BC
361+  6EEE F1          >                    POP     AF
361+  6EEF             >                ENDIF
362+  6EEF
363+  6EEF 3A 2E 7A             ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
364+  6EF2 B7                   or      a
365+  6EF3 C2 3B 71             jp      nz, .interpreter_error
366+  6EF6
367+  6EF6 2A 2F 7A             ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
368+  6EF9 CD 69 89             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintHex16
369+  6EFC 3E 0D                ld      a, 13
370+  6EFE CD 76 88             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar
371+  6F01 3E 0A                ld      a, 10
372+  6F03 CD 76 88             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar
373+  6F06 B7                   or      a
374+  6F07 C3 47 71             jp      .loader_restore_stack
375+  6F0A
376+  6F0A              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
377+  6F0A              ; Error exits. All return through the same original-stack restoration.
378+  6F0A              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
379+  6F0A              .diag_dma_error:
380+  6F0A                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_ipc_diag_dma_fail

381+  6F12 37                   scf

383+  6F16

388+  6F22

390+  6F22             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1

390+  6F29 E1          >                    POP     HL

395+  6F3E E1          >                    POP     HL

397+  6F42                      MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl

543+  7147              .loader_restore_stack:
544+  7147 2A 1B 7A             ld      hl, (P124_X86_LOADER_SP_BACKUP)
545+  714A F9                   ld      sp, hl
546+  714B C9                   ret
547+  714C
548+  714C              ; =========================================================================
549+  714C              ; NeoGS transport helpers — exact CBIT/DBIT meanings from Guide v0.1.A
550+  714C              ; =========================================================================
551+  714C
552+  714C              ; IN: HL=source in host RAM, BC=payload byte count (1..#2FFE).
553+  714C              ; OUT: Carry clear after NeoGS armed DMA, consumed the host end marker,
554+  714C              ;      and disabled DMA. Carry set on timeout.
555+  714C              ;
556+  714C              ; Protocol:
557+  714C              ;   host OUT GSCOM,CMD_LOAD_X86 -> CBIT=1
558+  714C              ;   NeoGS configures DMA, clears CLRCBIT -> CBIT=0 (READY)
559+  714C              ;   host LDIR to #0000..#3FFF (no dummy write)
560+  714C              ;   host OUT GSCOM,#FF -> CBIT=1 (end marker)
561+  714C              ;   NeoGS disables DMA, clears CLRCBIT -> CBIT=0 (DONE)
562+  714C              neogs_send_dma_block:
563+  714C 78                   ld      a, b
564+  714D B1                   or      c
565+  714E CA A6 71             jp      z, .dma_bad_length
566+  7151 78                   ld      a, b
567+  7152 FE 40                cp      #40
568+  7154 38 08                jr      c, .dma_length_ok
569+  7156 C2 A6 71             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
570+  7159 79                   ld      a, c
571+  715A B7                   or      a
572+  715B C2 A6 71             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
573+  715E              .dma_length_ok:
574+  715E 22 1D 7A             ld      (P124_DMA_SOURCE), hl
575+  7161 ED 43 1F 7A          ld      (P124_DMA_LENGTH), bc
576+  7165
577+  7165                      ; Validate payload + 2-byte length prefix <= $3000.
578+  7165 C5                   push    bc
579+  7166 E1                   pop     hl
580+  7167 11 02 00             ld      de, 2
581+  716A 19                   add     hl, de
582+  716B 7C                   ld      a, h
583+  716C FE 30                cp      #30
584+  716E 38 08                jr      c, .dma_total_length_ok
585+  7170 C2 A6 71             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
586+  7173 7D                   ld      a, l
587+  7174 B7                   or      a
588+  7175 C2 A6 71             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
589+  7178              .dma_total_length_ok:
590+  7178 3E 02                ld      a, CMD_LOAD_X86
591+  717A D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
592+  717C CD 5C 6B             call    neogs_wait_command_done
593+  717F D8                   ret     c
594+  7180
595+  7180                      ; LDIR is used for BOTH pieces of the frame. This is intentional:
596+  7180                      ; the DMA engine observes host memory-write cycles. The first LDIR
597+  7180                      ; writes the two-byte header to host addresses #0000-#0001; the second
598+  7180                      ; writes the payload to #0002 onward. No direct LD (nn),A writes and
599+  7180                      ; no temporary stack are needed.
600+  7180 F3                   di
601+  7181 2A 1F 7A             ld      hl, (P124_DMA_LENGTH)
602+  7184 22 21 7A             ld      (P124_DMA_HEADER), hl
603+  7187 21 21 7A             ld      hl, P124_DMA_HEADER
604+  718A 11 00 00             ld      de, DMA_HOST_WINDOW
605+  718D 01 02 00             ld      bc, 2
606+  7190 ED B0                ldir
607+  7192
608+  7192 2A 1D 7A             ld      hl, (P124_DMA_SOURCE)
609+  7195 11 02 00             ld      de, DMA_HOST_WINDOW + 2
610+  7198 ED 4B 1F 7A          ld      bc, (P124_DMA_LENGTH)
611+  719C ED B0                ldir
612+  719E
613+  719E                      ; End marker is a command-byte write; it is NOT a DMA data byte.
614+  719E 3E FF                ld      a, #FF
615+  71A0 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
616+  71A2 CD 5C 6B             call    neogs_wait_command_done
617+  71A5 C9                   ret
618+  71A6
619+  71A6              .dma_bad_length:
620+  71A6 37                   scf
621+  71A7 C9                   ret
622+  71A8
623+  71A8              ; Send RUN command and fetch the four-byte application result frame:
624+  71A8              ; A5 signature, status, AX low, AX high.
625+  71A8              neogs_run_and_get_result:
626+  71A8 3E 03                ld      a, CMD_RUN_X86
627+  71AA D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
628+  71AC CD C9 71             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
629+  71AF D8                   ret     c
630+  71B0 C3 48 72             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
631+  71B3
632+  71B3              ; Execute the actual TR-DOS task with the CS:IP real-mode core.
633+  71B3              neogs_run_x86_real_and_get_result:
634+  71B3 3E 0B                ld      a, CMD_RUN_X86_REAL
635+  71B5 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
636+  71B7 CD C9 71             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
637+  71BA D8                   ret     c
638+  71BB C3 48 72             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
639+  71BE
640+  71BE              ; Stage 3A guest BIOS video test. Its output is drained while the NeoGS
641+  71BE              ; command remains active; DBIT bytes are never confused with the result frame.
642+  71BE              neogs_bios_video_test_and_get_result:
643+  71BE 3E 0A                ld      a, CMD_BIOS_VIDEO_TEST
644+  71C0 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
645+  71C2 CD C9 71             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
646+  71C5 D8                   ret     c
647+  71C6 C3 48 72             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
648+  71C9
649+  71C9              ; Wait for the RUN command while servicing the bidirectional data channel.
650+  71C9              ; Stream protocol: nonzero=character, 00 00=clear, 00 01 row col=set cursor.
651+  71C9              ; Each character is sent to emulator printer port #EF and rendered on screen.
652+  71C9              neogs_wait_run_done_with_console:
653+  71C9 D5                   push    de
654+  71CA 16 10                ld      d, #10
655+  71CC              .run_outer:
656+  71CC 21 FF FF             ld      hl, IPC_CMD_TIMEOUT
657+  71CF              .run_inner:
658+  71CF DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
659+  71D1 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
660+  71D3 28 38                jr      z, .no_stream_byte
661+  71D5 DB B3                in      a, (GSDAT)
662+  71D7 B7                   or      a
663+  71D8 28 0C                jr      z, .stream_control
664+  71DA F5                   push    af
665+  71DB 01 EF 00             ld      bc, #00EF
666+  71DE ED 79                out     (c), a
667+  71E0 F1                   pop     af
668+  71E1 CD 76 88             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar
669+  71E4 18 E9                jr      .run_inner
670+  71E6
671+  71E6              .stream_control:
672+  71E6 CD 88 6B             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
673+  71E9 38 2E                jr      c, .run_timeout
674+  71EB B7                   or      a
675+  71EC 28 1A                jr      z, .stream_clear
676+  71EE FE 01                cp      1
677+  71F0 20 DD                jr      nz, .run_inner
678+  71F2 CD 88 6B             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
679+  71F5 38 22                jr      c, .run_timeout
680+  71F7 32 34 7A             ld      (P124_BIOS_STREAM_ROW), a
681+  71FA CD 88 6B             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
682+  71FD 38 1A                jr      c, .run_timeout
683+  71FF 4F                   ld      c, a
684+  7200 3A 34 7A             ld      a, (P124_BIOS_STREAM_ROW)
685+  7203 CD 68 88             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_SetCursor
686+  7206 18 C7                jr      .run_inner
687+  7208
688+  7208              .stream_clear:
689+  7208 CD 1D 88             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_Clear
690+  720B 18 C2                jr      .run_inner
691+  720D
692+  720D              .no_stream_byte:
693+  720D CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
694+  720F 28 0B                jr      z, .run_done
695+  7211 2B                   dec     hl
696+  7212 7C                   ld      a, h
697+  7213 B5                   or      l
698+  7214 20 B9                jr      nz, .run_inner
699+  7216 15                   dec     d
700+  7217 20 B3                jr      nz, .run_outer
701+  7219              .run_timeout:
702+  7219 D1                   pop     de
703+  721A 37                   scf
704+  721B C9                   ret
705+  721C              .run_done:
706+  721C D1                   pop     de
707+  721D B7                   or      a
708+  721E C9                   ret
709+  721F
710+  721F              ; Run the BIOS low-memory POST and fetch the existing four-byte result frame.
711+  721F              neogs_bios_post_and_get_result:
712+  721F 3E 07                ld      a, CMD_BIOS_POST
713+  7221 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
714+  7223 CD 6E 6B             call    neogs_wait_command_done_long
715+  7226 D8                   ret     c
716+  7227 C3 48 72             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
717+  722A
718+  722A              ; Run the 8086 interrupt entry/return architectural self-test.
719+  722A              neogs_int_test_and_get_result:
720+  722A 3E 08                ld      a, CMD_INT_TEST
721+  722C D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
722+  722E CD 6E 6B             call    neogs_wait_command_done_long
723+  7231 D8                   ret     c
724+  7232 C3 48 72             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
725+  7235
726+  7235              ; Run a real guest INT/IRET handler test and fetch the result frame.
727+  7235              neogs_int_guest_test_and_get_result:
728+  7235 3E 09                ld      a, CMD_INT_GUEST_TEST
729+  7237 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
730+  7239                      ; Drain NeoGS trace characters over DBIT while guest regression runs.
731+  7239                      ; The same host routine mirrors every byte to emulator port #EF.
732+  7239 CD C9 71             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
733+  723C D8                   ret     c
734+  723D C3 48 72             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
735+  7240
736+  7240              ; Run the NeoGS physical-memory self-test and fetch its result frame.
737+  7240              neogs_mem_test_and_get_result:
738+  7240 3E 05                ld      a, CMD_MEM_TEST
739+  7242 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
740+  7244 CD 6E 6B             call    neogs_wait_command_done_long
741+  7247 D8                   ret     c
742+  7248
743+  7248              neogs_get_result_frame:
744+  7248 3E 04                ld      a, CMD_GET_STATUS
745+  724A D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
746+  724C
747+  724C CD 88 6B             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
748+  724F D8                   ret     c
749+  7250 32 2D 7A             ld      (P124_X86_RESULT_MAGIC), a
750+  7253
751+  7253 CD 88 6B             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
752+  7256 D8                   ret     c
753+  7257 32 2E 7A             ld      (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS), a
754+  725A
755+  725A CD 88 6B             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
756+  725D D8                   ret     c
757+  725E 5F                   ld      e, a
758+  725F
759+  725F CD 88 6B             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
760+  7262 D8                   ret     c
761+  7263 57                   ld      d, a
762+  7264 ED 53 2F 7A          ld      (P124_X86_RESULT_AX), de
763+  7268 ED 53 03 65          ld      (X86_RESULT_AX), de
764+  726C
765+  726C CD 5C 6B             call    neogs_wait_command_done
766+  726F D8                   ret     c
767+  7270
768+  7270 3A 2D 7A             ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_MAGIC)
769+  7273 FE A5                cp      NGS_RESULT_SIGNATURE
770+  7275 28 02                jr      z, .result_frame_ok
771+  7277 37                   scf
772+  7278 C9                   ret
773+  7279              .result_frame_ok:
774+  7279 B7                   or      a
775+  727A C9                   ret
776+  727B

  1+  8A67              ; =========================================================================
  2+  8A67              ; FILE: zx_main/file_utils.asm
  3+  8A67              ; PROJECT 124 — TR-DOS directory utilities
  4+  8A67              ; Sector API verified for TR-DOS 5.04T: C=#05, D=track, E=internal sector
  5+  8A67              ; 0..15, B=count, HL=destination; error status at #5D0F.
  6+  8A67              ; =========================================================================
  7+  8A67
  8+  8A67              ; IN: CursorY = visible row (2..10)
  9+  8A67              ; OUT: HL = 16-byte descriptor in TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER, carry clear.
 10+  8A67              ;      HL=0 and carry set for an invalid/empty row.
 11+  8A67              GetSelectedFileDescriptor:
 11+  8A67
 12+  8A67 3A 18 80             ld      a, (CursorY)
 13+  8A6A FE 02                cp      2
 14+  8A6C 38 1C                jr      c, .bad_row
 15+  8A6E FE 0B                cp      11
 16+  8A70 30 18                jr      nc, .bad_row
 17+  8A72 D6 02                sub     2
 18+  8A74 6F                   ld      l, a
 19+  8A75 26 00                ld      h, 0
 20+  8A77 29                   add     hl, hl
 21+  8A78 29                   add     hl, hl
 22+  8A79 29                   add     hl, hl
 23+  8A7A 29                   add     hl, hl
 24+  8A7B 11 00 C0             ld      de, TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER
 25+  8A7E 19                   add     hl, de
 26+  8A7F
 27+  8A7F                      ; Reject an empty/deleted/invalid catalogue slot.
 28+  8A7F 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
 29+  8A80 FE 20                cp      32
 30+  8A82 38 06                jr      c, .bad_row
 31+  8A84 FE 7F                cp      127
 32+  8A86 30 02                jr      nc, .bad_row
 33+  8A88 B7                   or      a
 34+  8A89 C9                   ret
 35+  8A8A              .bad_row:
 36+  8A8A 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
 37+  8A8D 37                   scf
 38+  8A8E C9                   ret

 57+  8AE2 AF                   xor     a

 60+  8AE9 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
 61+  8AEC

 63+  8AEC 11 00 00             ld      de, #0000

163+  8B8E E5                   push    hl

200+  8BD1 FE 0B                cp      11
201+  8BD3 D2 F5 8B             jp      nc, .directory_done
202+  8BD6
203+  8BD6                      ; Advance to next 16-byte descriptor. Restore saved slot counter.
204+  8BD6 E1                   pop     hl
205+  8BD7 11 10 00             ld      de, 16
206+  8BDA 19                   add     hl, de
207+  8BDB C1                   pop     bc
208+  8BDC 05                   dec     b
209+  8BDD C2 0E 8B             jp      nz, .loop_slots
210+  8BE0 C3 F7 8B             jp      .directory_success
211+  8BE3
212+  8BE3              .invalid_slot:
213+  8BE3                      ; Drop the per-slot saves, advance HL, and decrement the ORIGINAL
214+  8BE3                      ; 128-entry counter. Do not load 16 into BC (that destroys B), and
215+  8BE3                      ; do not push the per-slot saves again before re-entering the loop.
216+  8BE3 E1                   pop     hl
217+  8BE4 C1                   pop     bc
218+  8BE5 11 10 00             ld      de, 16
219+  8BE8 19                   add     hl, de
220+  8BE9 05                   dec     b
221+  8BEA C2 0E 8B             jp      nz, .loop_slots
222+  8BED C3 F7 8B             jp      .directory_success
223+  8BF0
224+  8BF0              .empty_slot:
225+  8BF0                      ; Per-slot BC/HL saves are still present.
226+  8BF0 E1                   pop     hl
227+  8BF1 C1                   pop     bc
228+  8BF2 C3 F7 8B             jp      .directory_success
229+  8BF5
230+  8BF5              .directory_done:
231+  8BF5                      ; Per-slot BC/HL saves are still present.
232+  8BF5 E1                   pop     hl
233+  8BF6 C1                   pop     bc

316   E195                      PAGE    0                       ; Возвращаем базовый нулевой банк памяти хоста

318   E195                      ; Синхронный экспорт бинарных блоков в образ TRD
319   E195                      SAVETRD "disk_A.trd", "proj124.C", 24576, ProgramEnd - 24576
320   E195                      SAVETRD "disk_A.trd", "boot.B", BASIC_START, BASIC_END - BASIC_START
321   E195                      ; One logical neoprog.C package, appended in 16-KiB chunks from
322   E195                      ; separate Spectrum RAM banks. Chunk boundaries are sector-aligned.
323   E195                      ASSERT NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK_COUNT >= 1
324   E195                      ASSERT NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK_COUNT <= 4
325   E195                      ASSERT NEOGS_PACKAGE_TOTAL_SIZE <= #FF00
326   E195
327   E195                      PAGE    1
328   E195                      ORG     #C000
329   C000              neoprog_chunk0_start:
330   C000                      INCBIN "zx_main/neoprog_part0.bin"
331   F481              neoprog_chunk0_end:
332   F481                      ASSERT neoprog_chunk0_end - neoprog_chunk0_start = NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK0_SIZE
333   F481                      SAVETRD "disk_A.trd", "neoprog.C", #C000, NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK0_SIZE
334   F481
335   F481                      IF NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK_COUNT > 1
336   F481 ~                    PAGE    3
337   F481 ~                    ORG     #C000
338   F481 ~            neoprog_chunk1_start:
339   F481 ~                    INCBIN "zx_main/neoprog_part1.bin"
340   F481 ~            neoprog_chunk1_end:

342   F481 ~                    SAVETRD "disk_A.trd",&"neoprog.C", #C000, NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK1_SIZE
343   F481                      ENDIF
344   F481
345   F481                      IF NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK_COUNT > 2
346   F481 ~                    PAGE    4
347   F481 ~                    ORG     #C000
348   F481 ~            neoprog_chunk2_start:

353   F481                      ENDIF
